### 2026-10-25 21:45:02  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #SSE Composite #Impact bullish #WTI #US10Y #Impact bearish

【Broker note: A-share indicators approaching historical lows】After the recent sharp sell-off, multiple A-share indicators are near historical troughs: volumes and turnover have collapsed to cycle lows, while ETF-led medium-to-long-term inflows have accelerated and stabilization efforts have intensified. Stabilization mechanisms are becoming more institutionalized, reinforcing a policy floor. Market bottoms tend to lead fundamental bottoms; corporate earnings and PPI remain on an improving trend, which is supportive for A-shares. External risks are limited by marked deleveraging in Japan and Korea, but US equity AI rerating appears shifting from investment expansion toward capital-return validation; together with elevated US Treasury yields and rising oil, this constrains easing and keeps higher rates a cap on valuations. Given historical precedence that ETF-supported troughs often precede rebounds, expect near-term range-bound bottoming with structural opportunities. Maintain balanced allocations and monitor key catalysts over the next two weeks. For the next 3–6 months: in a high-rate environment prioritize companies with strong earnings/cashflow (the 'numerator') and healthy shareholding/liquidity structures; when rates roll over, broaden exposure to technology; await further domestic policy follow-through to shift positioning toward domestic-demand beneficiaries.

### 2026-10-25 21:42:38  #資料 #市場

【招商策略：A股多項指標已接近歷史底部區間】招商策略研報認為，近期市場快速下跌後，A股多項指標已接近歷史底部區間：成交明顯縮量、換手率降至接近歷史大底水平，以ETF為代表的中長線資金加速入市、穩市力度加大；穩市機制制度化特徵增強，政策底逐步夯實；市場底往往領先基本面底，當前盈利與PPI仍處改善趨勢，對A股仍呈正面驅動。外部壓力方面，日韓市場槓桿明顯去化，海外傳導風險可控；但美股AI定價正由“投入擴張”轉向“資本回報驗證”，疊加美債利率高位、油價上行壓縮寬鬆空間，高利率對估值仍有約束。考慮到ETF放量託底後歷史上市場反彈機率較高，短期或以震盪築底和結構性機會為主，建議維持均衡配置並重點關注未來兩週的重要節點。未來3—6個月行業配置的核心思路在於：高利率階段關注分子端的優勢，其次看籌碼結構是否健康；利率轉跌優先考慮科技寬度；待後續國內政策接力再進一步向內需擴散。

【Weekly preview: Super central-bank week, big-tech earnings; China plenum, US core PCE, Israel vote, OPEC+】Week ahead: a packed central-bank calendar and major tech earnings should drive rates, liquidity and risk appetite. China’s 20th CPC Fifth Plenum meets in Beijing Oct 26–29; market participants should watch for policy signals. Monday: Germany IFO and US Dallas Fed business index; Taiwan and New Zealand exchanges closed — limited regional liquidity; Hong Kong/US earnings include Verizon and selected HK small-caps. Tuesday: US ADP, durable goods, FHFA house-price data, Conference Board consumer confidence and Richmond Fed; 900bn yuan 1‑year MLF matures (PBOC operation watch); Israel holds national election; Boeing, Coca‑Cola and UPS report premarket; SK Hynix, Robinhood and NXP report after close. Wednesday: weekly API/EIA crude inventories; Australia CPI; RBNZ rate decision; Bank of Canada rate decision with Governor MACKLEM press briefing; RBA officials testify to Senate; UK chancellor unveils his first budget; Alphabet and Microsoft report after the US close. Thursday: US FOMC decision and KEVIN WARSH press conference; US advance Q3 GDP, core PCE (inflation), personal spending and weekly initial claims — all market-moving for pricing of US rates; ECB decision and LAGARDE press conference; US weekly EIA natural‑gas stocks; China domestic refined-fuel price window opens; Apple event; Amazon, Intel and SanDisk report after the close. Friday: BoJ policy decision and Governor Ueda briefing; US employment‑cost index (Q3) and Chicago PMI; Eurozone and major European GDP/CPI releases; Canada GDP; major oil majors report premarket (BP, Exxon, Chevron). Saturday: US rig count; China official manufacturing PMI (Oct). Sunday: North American clocks roll back one hour (DST end) — US/Canada market open and data schedules shift; seven OPEC+ producers meet to set December output policy.

### 2026-10-25 20:01:56  #公司 #市場

【記憶體價格飆升給PC市場帶來寒意 第三季度全球出貨量驟降21%】受人工智慧（AI）需求增長的推動，近期記憶體價格大幅上漲，導致PC需求出現明顯下滑。據市場研究機構Counterpoint Research釋出的資料，今年第三季度全球PC出貨量為5700萬臺，較去年同期下降21%；與上一季度相比，也下滑了13.8%。據Counterpoint分析，儘管存在更換舊裝置及遷移至新版 Windows 作業系統的需求，但消費者仍推遲了個人電腦的購買計劃，導致整體出貨量大幅下滑。從廠商表現來看，聯想的出貨量為1500萬臺，較去年同期下降了23%，但該公司以26.3%的市場份額保持市場領先地位。惠普的市場份額位居第二，出貨量為1030萬臺，較上年下降了31%。

### 2026-10-25 20:01:37  #MKT News #MU #Impact mixed #HPQ.O #Impact bearish #0992.HK

【Memory price surge chills PC market; global Q3 shipments fall 21% YoY】Memory prices have jumped on AI-driven demand, denting PC demand, Counterpoint Research says. Global PC shipments in Q3 totaled 57.0 mln units, down 21% YoY and 13.8% QoQ. Counterpoint says consumers delayed purchases despite replacement demand and migration to new Windows, causing the volume drop. Vendor detail: Lenovo shipped 15.0 mln units, down 23% YoY with a 26.3% share; HP shipped 10.3 mln units, down 31% YoY and ranked second.

### 2026-10-25 19:58:33  #MKT News #Commodities #Precious Metals #XAUUSD

【Gold mine collapse in eastern Sudan kills 2, injures 3】On Oct. 25 local time Sudan Mineral Resources Company said a collapse at an abandoned, closed shaft in Gedaref state in eastern Sudan killed two miners and injured three. The company said the shaft had been ordered closed but some miners entered illegally to extract gold; an investigation into the cause and circumstances is under way. Sudan is a leading African gold producer and exporter and gold is a major source of foreign exchange; production relies heavily on artisanal methods and limited safety infrastructure, making mine collapses and operational disruptions a recurring risk to output.

【Apollo economist: AI has yet to deliver returns to US firms outside tech】Apollo Global Management chief economist Torsten Slok said that despite heavy corporate AI spending, AI has so far produced no measurable returns for US firms outside the tech sector. He noted per-employee revenue is rising at the seven largest tech companies but flat across the other 493 S&P 500 firms, and profit margins show the same divergence, with no evidence AI is boosting productivity. Slok said mainstream US companies are cutting investment and treating AI token-consumption costs as a cost center rather than a growth driver, which is squeezing margins. As US earnings season opens, investors will watch corporate AI spending and whether it delivers promised payback. Last week Meta said it would further ramp AI bets and raise capex plans, prompting market jitters; Meta shares fell and its credit-risk measures hit record highs.

【CAICT report: China daily token consumption about 500 trillion in June 2026】China Academy of Information and Communications Technology (CAICT) said in its 2026 Large Model Token Service Performance Monitoring Report that domestic daily token consumption was about 500 trillion in June 2026, nearly four times December 2025. In August the average across domestic models was 78.23 tokens/sec (TPS), roughly double the 2025 average of 38.25 TPS, but below a representative foreign model at 112.82 TPS. 【Three drivers behind gold's recent decline】Spot gold briefly fell below $4,000/oz in Asian trade on Oct. 23 — the first time since July — and is down more than 3% this week. Wang Jun, chief analyst at Green Dahua Futures, cites three market-moving drivers: (1) high oil prices — Brent topped $114/bbl, reinforcing global and US inflation persistence; (2) firmer Fed December rate-hike expectations, with elevated US inflation lifting the dollar and US Treasury yields and eroding gold’s appeal (Wang calls this the main driver); and (3) global liquidity tightening and deleveraging. He expects international gold to remain under pressure into late October and early November.

【第九屆世界投資論壇在卡達多哈開幕】當地時間10月25日，由聯合國貿易和發展會議（貿發會議）與卡達共同舉辦的第九屆世界投資論壇在卡達首都多哈開幕。來自各國政府、國際組織、企業及投資機構的代表齊聚多哈，圍繞全球經濟不確定性加劇背景下的國際投資合作、可持續發展等議題展開討論。本屆論壇為期三天，以“投資未來”為主題，重點關注全球產業鏈供應鏈重構、地緣政治緊張局勢、發展中國家融資需求，以及投資促進和平與可持續發展等議題。與會代表還將就加強多邊合作、推動地區經濟一體化、支援衝突地區恢復重建，以及促進人工智慧和綠色能源等領域投資進行交流，探討如何透過國際合作應對全球經濟挑戰。（央視新聞）

【崔東樹：2026年9月中國汽車進口2.1萬輛降49%】2026年9月進口車進口2.1萬輛，同比下滑49%，環比下降6%，降幅驚人。2026年1-9月進口汽車29萬輛，同比下降20%。今年汽車進口結構中的乘用車佔到超99%的絕對主力地位，如果包含輕卡中的皮卡，則乘用車數量佔比更高。2026年1-9月進口混動增15%的表現很好，純電動乘用車降27%，插混降54%，進口新能源乘用車表現較弱。 乘用車的進口車型排量集中於2升以下汽油車型，去年3-4升抗跌能力較好，今年1.5-2升相對較好。9月進口車下滑壓力仍較大。2026年9月進口最高的前10國家是：日本7399輛、德國7255輛、英國2488輛、斯洛伐克1418輛、墨西哥818輛、美國791輛、奧地利355輛、比利時112輛、韓國95輛、義大利90輛，其中本期較同期增量增大的前五個是：墨西哥307輛、奧地利107輛、比利時42輛、韓國41輛、法國16輛。2026年1-9月進口最高的前10國家是：日本136652輛、德國66438輛、美國28350輛、斯洛伐克20422輛、英國18803輛、墨西哥4974輛、奧地利4193輛、泰國2270輛、義大利786輛、韓國760輛，其中本期較同期增量增大的前五個是：泰國2027輛、奧地利1948輛、墨西哥1576輛、中國540輛、南非373輛。

The firm views the move as sentiment repair rather than a profit-driven broad reversal and expects the Shanghai Composite to form a short-term phase bottom near 3,700. 【部分QDII專戶產品陸續清盤？業內人士：與QDII專戶額度比例調整有關】近日，有公募機構人士向記者透露，自家公司的部分專戶產品陸續清盤，而這些產品基本為QDII類專戶產品。該人士透露，這一動作與公司在公募和專戶兩類產品上QDII投資額度的比例調整有關。年初，多家基金公司收到通知，須調整QDII投資額度在公募產品與專戶產品上的使用比例，在2027年底之前將用於專戶的QDII投資額度調整至20%以內。圍繞著這一規則，各家基金公司的QDII專戶調整步伐已在加速進行中。有業內人士分析，是否將專戶QDII投資額度壓降到20%以內，可能影響到後續額度的申請。“前段時間新一批的QDII額度我們公司沒有申請到，產品部覺得與壓降工作還沒有完成有較大關係。”來自北京基金公司的一位產品部人士向記者透露。當然，也有不少公司QDII投資額度的原本分配比例已符合相關規定。“我們公司原本在專戶方面的額度就低於20%，所以目前沒有相關動作。”一位基金經理向記者表示，“QDII的額度本就具備一定稀缺性。從中長期角度看，讓更多公募投資者享受到多元投資的便利，符合普惠金融的投資理念。”(中證金牛座)

【Tanker freight surge, benchmark pricing dispute leads to suit against 'Dow Jones of shipping'】Mercuria, one of the world's largest commodity traders, has sued the 282‑year‑old Baltic Exchange; the UK High Court will hear the case on Monday. The dispute centers on whether the Baltic should have continued publishing its flagship tanker route benchmark TD3C for voyages via the Strait of Hormuz after US and Israel opened hostilities with Iran. Mercuria says voyages fell sharply and Iran declared a blockade, and argued the Baltic should have suspended TD3C; it initially alleged losses of "hundreds of millions of dollars" but amended its filing to drop monetary damages and now seeks declaratory relief that publications since early March are invalid. The Baltic Exchange says short‑term inactivity on a route is not uncommon, brokers continued to provide rates, and warns a Mercuria victory would have "chaotic market‑wide consequences" and could force the unwinding of hundreds of trades. TD3C underpins pricing and hedging for billions of dollars of freight derivatives.

【DianKe Siyi to unveil higher‑frequency vector network analyzer and test suite at Shenzhen Semiconductor Expo】At an Oct. 24 earnings briefing, DianKe Siyi said it will launch a higher‑frequency, higher‑performance vector network analyzer (VNA) and a series of PCB test solutions at the Shenzhen Semiconductor Expo. The company is shifting PCB offerings from R&D and OEM lab use toward automated production‑line testing to meet high‑volume manufacturing needs. Revenue from production‑side PCB testing is currently small. PCB test coverage includes insertion loss, impedance and dielectric constant, and current sample validations are broadly in line with expectations.

【China SASAC urges accelerated SOE reform and stronger penetrating supervision】On Oct. 23 the State-owned Assets Supervision and Administration Commission (SASAC) held a central study session and instructed SOEs to align roles with new-era priorities and accelerate high-quality development. SASAC called for full implementation of the new development concept, optimization of the state-sector footprint, intensified R&D on key core technologies to boost SOE core functions and competitiveness, and stronger strategic support from the state sector. It urged rapid advancement and firm implementation of deeper SOE reforms to remove institutional barriers to high-quality enterprise development. SASAC also pushed to raise regulatory effectiveness by transforming oversight methods, accelerating penetrating supervision, improving the legal and institutional framework for state-asset supervision, and integrating supervisory channels to enhance professionalism, systemization, rule-of-law and efficiency. 【法國經濟拉響警報 物價股市國債全亂套】最近，法國的麻煩好像一下子全冒出來了。自本月17日起，法國多地爆發大規模抗議，部分地區還出現了嚴重的暴力衝突。物價上漲，國債收益率飆升還遭到拋售，股市也承壓。法國經濟到底怎麼了？法國政府常年入不敷出，缺口只能靠借錢填補。官方資料顯示，截至今年6月底，法國公共債務佔GDP比重達到119%，是80年來的最高水平。 市場最擔心的是：法國還要借多少錢？未來又能不能還得上？這種擔憂，直觀地體現在借債成本上。法國國債近期持續遭拋售，10年期國債收益率本週一度衝破5%，最高觸達5.14%附近，這是2002年以來從未有過的。多家權威機構指出，法國要想擺脫目前的困境，短期看，需要拿出可信的財政整頓方案，重建市場信心。長期看，還得依靠經濟增長拉動。但這並不容易實現，法國政府已經將今年的經濟增長預期從此前本就不高的0.7%下調到0.5%，這已經是法國政府今年內第三次下調全年增長預期了。（央視財經）

### 2026-10-25 11:23:58  #MKT News #7203.T #Impact bullish #7201.T #7269.T

【Japanese automakers accelerate R&D cycles to speed model launches】Kyodo reports Japanese automakers are accelerating vehicle R&D to keep pace with rivals that launch new models faster. Toyota, Honda, Nissan and peers typically take about 4–5 years to develop a new model. Nissan's new family-style development strategy will use a common platform across multiple same-size models, covering roughly 80% of global sales and expected to cut parts varieties by about 70%. Toyota's Area 35 plan aims to reduce parts counts and raise factory area utilization by 35%. Suzuki plans to halve development time to about two years; Mitsubishi targets roughly a 20% reduction to a three-year cycle by around 2030.

【Nomura maintains Bank of Korea terminal rate at 3.50%, sees 25bp hikes in Nov 2026 and Feb 2027】Nomura says the Bank of Korea’s unanimous October hold was expected and a November rate hike remains its base case. The BOK will likely raise its growth and inflation forecasts in November, but Nomura judges the bank’s policy guidance is more hawkish than its economic outlook. Limited interventions help explain the BOK’s cautious stance. Weak household income and consumption have left MPC members divided over inflation persistence and the need for further tightening. Nomura keeps a 3.50% terminal rate forecast and projects 25bp hikes in Nov 2026 and Feb 2027. Sluggish household purchasing power and a stronger won may cap inflation; November’s forecasts and the dot plot will test market support for a higher peak.

【三星電子會長李在鎔近日出訪阿聯酋】三星電子會長李在鎔近日訪問阿聯酋。該地區目前處於近乎戰時的地緣緊張環境，分析師稱，此行將推動三星集團在中東業務提速，涵蓋面向人工智慧資料中心建設與運營的先進晶片供應，以及聯合迴圈電站專案的拓展。這是李在鎔近一年來首次到訪中東，他此行首站考察阿爾達法拉燃氣電廠專案。該專案由三星物產與阿聯酋建築商特洛伊總承包公司組建聯合體中標，總投資13.5億美元（約合1.8萬億韓元），計劃明年投產。阿爾達法拉電廠建成後可為AI資料中心提供約1吉瓦電力，是阿聯酋國家級“AI轉型（AX）”戰略的核心基礎設施。李在鎔和阿聯酋高層已經建立穩固人脈，業內期待他能在當前近乎戰時的複雜環境下推進業務，拿下訂單、落實投資。

【五糧液普五零售均價大漲逼近800元 飛天茅臺持平 洋河夢之藍M6+四連陰跌破600元 習酒君品重跌】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，10月25日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價四漲七跌一平，下跌品種仍佔多數，整體價盤連續第四日下行，但數值跌幅較前幾日明顯收窄。上漲端，五糧液普五八代大漲7元至796元，結束此前低位橫盤強勢反彈，重新向800元關口靠攏；青花汾20上漲1元至385元，終結此前四連陰，近期持續下探頹勢暫獲緩解；古井貢古20上漲5元至518元，低位反彈力度強勁，成為今日漲幅靠前的單品。下跌端，精品茅臺下跌3元至2452元，五連陰持續承壓，價格進一步重新整理近期低位；茅臺1935小幅下跌1元至684元，昨日小幅抬升後漲幅回吐；國窖1573下跌2元至883元，連續第二日下滑，已跌至近期波動區間的下沿；洋河夢之藍M6+下跌3元至597元，四連陰後跌破600元整數關口，再度重新整理近一個月低位；習酒君品下跌4元至633元，前一日反彈未能延續，價格再度逼近近期低點；水晶劍南春下跌2元至409元，短暫反彈後重新跌破410元關口。持平方面，飛天茅臺維持1790元不變，二連陽後暫事修整；五糧液1618保持826元，三連跌後進入橫盤整理；青花郎維持685元不變，結束此前長影四連陰，暫時止住連續下滑勢頭。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價小幅下移，雖然六款產品價格下跌，但五糧液普五八代大幅上漲7元、古井貢古20上漲5元，對整體價盤形成一定支撐，使總價跌幅明顯收窄。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10658元，較昨日下跌2元，連續第五日下行，並創下近39天以來最低紀錄。

【生態環境部就地下水汙染防治重點區管理徵求意見】生態環境部正在就地下水汙染防治重點區環境管理向社會公開徵求意見，公眾可以在10月31日前，向生態環境部反饋意見。本次徵求意見稿 力求透過分割槽管控、源頭防控，築牢地下水飲用水安全屏障。這份《關於加強地下水汙染防治重點區環境管理的指導意見（徵求意見稿）》，將地下水水質好、防汙染能力弱的重點區劃分為保護類、管控類兩類。保護類主要是地下水飲用水源保護區、補給區等；管控類是開發價值高、地下水脆弱的區域。檔案提出階段目標：到2028年重點區管理體系初步形成，風險得到初步管控；到2030年管控體系全面落地，地下水環境質量穩中向好。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-25 07:59:46  #市場 #國際

【分析：本輪燃料油價格上漲有四大支撐 燃料油價格短期偏強】方正中期期貨研究院能化研究員郭豔鵬用“先抑後揚”概括本週燃料油價格的走勢。在他看來，本輪燃料油價格上漲有四大支撐。其一，中東、俄羅斯兩大燃料油核心出口地供應同步收縮，兩地合計出口佔全球六成以上。10月以來霍爾木茲海峽通航受阻，波斯灣成品油出口較正常水平低約30%；俄羅斯煉廠頻繁遭遇襲擊，原油加工量下滑，燃料油出口從去年的約90萬桶/日回落至50萬桶/日附近。其二，物流成本居高不下，歐洲到新加坡套利視窗關閉，外部補充貨源稀缺。其三，航運轉運、煉廠結構調整帶來雙重需求支撐，上游減壓渣油優先保障柴油、汽油供應，分流燃料油資源。其四，新加坡、富查伊拉燃料油庫存處於近年低位，價格緩衝空間很小。展望後市，郭豔鵬認為，燃料油價格短期偏強，向上彈性更大。當前美伊博弈進入關鍵視窗期，地緣局勢的不確定性顯著上升，疊加原油、燃料油庫存偏低，市場對供應擾動的反應會被放大，行情反覆、寬幅波動的機率加大。（期貨日報）

【美國務院更新中東多國旅行警告 稱胡塞和伊朗襲擊威脅增加】據美國媒體24日報道，因葉門胡塞武裝和伊朗的襲擊威脅增加，美國國務院針對13箇中東國家更新了旅行警告。報道說，此次更新涉及的國家和地區包括巴林、伊朗、伊拉克、以色列、約旦、科威特、黎巴嫩、阿曼、卡達、沙烏地阿拉伯、敘利亞、阿聯酋和葉門。美駐上述國家的大使館日前在其官網上釋出最新旅行警告稱，預警級別及風險指標均未發生變化，但預警摘要已更新。其中，美駐沙特大使館釋出旅行警告稱，“伊朗支援的胡塞武裝發動潛在武裝襲擊的可能性有所增加，同時伊朗正試圖針對該地區的美國政府官員採取行動”。美駐其他國家大使館的旅行警告則只提到胡塞武裝襲擊威脅增加。（新華社）

【Amgen drug with AstraZeneca immunotherapy substantially extends survival in extensive‑stage small‑cell lung cancer】Conference data published Saturday show Amgen’s Imdelltra combined with AstraZeneca’s Imfinzi cut the risk of death by 58% versus Imfinzi alone in extensive‑stage small‑cell lung cancer. Median overall survival was 21.1 months for the combo versus 12.7 months for Imfinzi monotherapy. Imfinzi is currently the post‑chemotherapy standard for patients without progression after first‑line chemo. Amgen CMO Paul Burton said earlier use of Imdelltra produced greater clinical benefit and called the result unprecedented for advancing treatment in this indication.

Crews set up on-site command, reopened distribution lines and restored power to shelters, tents and prefabricated housing, following an on-site “build one, energize one” procedure. He said equities have upside repair potential provided earnings show marginal stabilization and liquidity expectations modestly recover. He advised against panic selling and recommended focusing on high‑quality names where earnings and valuation align, maintaining rational, patient positioning. After the recent correction, broad and sector indices have dropped to historically low percentiles; some growth subsectors sit near one‑year lows, and financials and cyclical value sectors are near historical troughs. 【《AIGC時代網際網路廣告治理藍皮書》釋出，倡導“以AI治AI”】10月24日，第33屆中國國際廣告節期間，《AIGC時代網際網路廣告治理藍皮書》正式釋出。 當前，AIGC技術帶來效率提升的另一面是風險升級：黑灰產藉助AI批次生成高擬真素材、偽造權威背書，僅靠人工稽核已難以應對。面對這些新風險，藍皮書給出貫穿廣告投放全過程的治理方法：將風險分為內容違規、技術對抗、交易鏈路、新場景四類，並從八個方面提出建議。 例如，藍皮書呈現了“以AI治AI”的實踐方法：治理的第一步是看清風險。利用多模態大模型同時看圖、讀字、聽聲，不僅能懂表面意思，還能聯絡上下文判定是否違規。巨量引擎商業安全內容安全策略負責人蕭濤介紹，依託智慧稽核系統，90%的素材最快10分鐘內完成稽核，效率提升75%。 此外，AI應用平臺也把使用者反饋納入治理，對老年人和未成年人專項保障。老年人可“零門檻”舉證、直達人工客服，家長用標準化模板反映問題，部分問題當天即可解決。 標準統一之後，平臺保持常態化風險監測和巡查，圍繞高發問題提前研判風險趨勢；針對改頭換面的新型變體，快速識別、及時更新攔截策略，推動識別與處置前置；同時持續迭代識別模型與稽核規則，對惡意造假、屢罰屢犯的主體從嚴處置直至封停清退，涉嫌違法犯罪的依法向有關部門報案或配合查處。（中新網）

【Blue Book on Internet Advertising Governance in the AIGC Era released, advocates "AI to govern AI"】Oct 24 — The Blue Book on Internet Advertising Governance in the AIGC Era was released at the 33rd China International Advertising Festival. It warns AIGC heightens ad-fraud and compliance risk as black/grey-market actors use AI to mass-produce hyper-real creative and fake authoritative endorsements, outstripping manual review. The paper frames risks as content violations, technical adversarial attacks, transaction-chain vulnerabilities and new scenarios, and sets out lifecycle governance across eight areas, endorsing an "AI to govern AI" approach. It highlights multimodal large models that jointly process images, text and audio to judge context and detect violations. Xiao Tao, head of content security strategy at Ocean Engine, said an intelligent review system now clears 90% of creatives in as fast as 10 minutes, boosting review efficiency by roughly 75%.

【MOFCOM spokesman responds to U.S. proposed outbound investment restrictions】On Oct. 23 the U.S. Treasury published a proposed rule tightening outbound investment restrictions and seeking public comment. 【萊寶高科：公司2026年車載觸控式螢幕業務板塊整體銷售收入可能同比下降】10月24日，萊寶高科在最新披露的投資者關係活動記錄表中表示，車載蓋板玻璃和車載觸控式螢幕2026年一方面仍然面臨產品價格持續下降的壓力，國內汽車市場內卷式降價對相關供應鏈的傳導作用對公司車載觸控式螢幕產品板塊營收和盈利能力的不利影響日益顯現，2026年上半年車載蓋板玻璃銷量和銷售收入同比去年同期已發生一定程度的下降，2026年下半年目前來看仍面臨價格下降的壓力；一方面還面臨已佔據主流市場地位的In Cell結構觸控顯示一體化帶來日益增加的替代競爭壓力，公司2026年車載觸控式螢幕業務板塊的整體銷售收入可能同比下降，且對該業務板塊的盈利能力造成一定的不利影響，敬請各位投資者予以客觀理性看待，具體經營業績請以公司後續披露的定期報告資料為準。

【Monetary Authority of Singapore, Bank Negara Malaysia and Bank of Thailand join BLOOM to advance cross-border digital-currency payments】The Monetary Authority of Singapore (MAS) said on the 23rd that Bank Negara Malaysia and the Bank of Thailand have formally joined the BLOOM cross-border digital currency cooperation initiative. The three central banks will work with industry to make cross-border transfers faster and more convenient. Cooperation will focus on: studying cross-border acceptance and settlement of digital currencies; promoting interoperability between national payment systems; and strengthening identity verification, transaction screening and risk management to secure cross-border transactions. Participant list has expanded to include Mastercard, Visa, Ripple, MAYBANK, Airwallex, Monee, Nium, ShopeePay and the World Bank Group (WBG).

【High-quality brain–computer interface dataset project launched in Wuhan】A high-quality brain–computer interface (BCI) dataset project led by Tongji Hospital of Huazhong University of Science and Technology was launched in Wuhan on the 24th. The hospital said it will build the dataset in partnership with leading domestic hospitals and core enterprises; the project is planned to be completed and operational in 2027. The dataset will target 10 clinical areas — stroke rehabilitation, spinal cord injury, Parkinson’s disease, epilepsy, disorders of consciousness, refractory pain, facial paralysis, speech-function disorders, movement-disorder diseases and gastrointestinal neuroregulation — covering primary clinical scenarios for neuromodulation and functional restoration. The programme will assemble six data modalities, prioritising brain-signal acquisition, intention decoding, functional assessment, closed-loop neuromodulation and rehabilitation-interaction multimodal datasets.

RTX 5080 launched near 8,000 yuan; channel quotes have risen to ~17,000 yuan from ~12,000 yuan two weeks ago (a ~4,000–5,000 yuan increase).

【企業微信回應少量賬號髮色情資訊】10月24日，少量企業微信賬號向客戶傳送疑似色情引流資訊。 隨後，企業微信釋出公告，“經企業微信安全團隊排查，確認為某第三方外掛軟體服務被攻破，導致使用該外掛服務的賬號受到影響。我們已將受影響的賬號強制下線，並封禁相關引流連結。 此次事件再次敲響警鐘：以“賬號多開”、“群控”等為噱頭的外掛軟體，存在巨大的安全隱患和隱私風險。使用此類服務，可能導致賬號被他人操控、企業資料和客戶資訊洩露，危及企業正常經營，損害客戶權益。所謂的“效率提升”，不能以犧牲安全和信任為代價。 為保護企業和使用者的合法權益，維護安全、健康的平臺生態，企業微信將進一步加大對外掛軟體的打擊力度： 一、持續排查和清理外掛軟體。加強對篡改客戶端、繞過平臺安全機制及使用限制等外掛行為的識別和處置，持續打擊外掛軟體及其開發、銷售行為，並依法追究相關主體的責任。 二、嚴肅處理使用外掛的違規行為。對使用外掛軟體的賬號，企業微信將依據平臺相關協議和規範，視違規情節採取相應處理措施。請正在使用外掛的企業和使用者立即停用相關軟體及服務，排查賬號安全隱患，避免企業經營和客戶服務受到進一步影響。 企業賬號承載著企業長期積累的客戶關係和信任。我們呼籲企業、員工和服務商共同拒絕外掛，安全、規範地使用賬號。 企業微信將堅決、持續打擊外掛軟體，切實維護企業和使用者的合法權益。”

【瑞士股市成為市場AI恐慌下的避風港】在歐洲股市震盪之際，瑞士一眾防禦屬性極強的大型龍頭股成為一抹亮色。瑞士市場指數跟蹤該國20只流動性最好的大盤股，本月累計上漲4.6%；而歐元區對標指數歐洲斯托克50指數同期下跌3.2%。瑞士股指近8個百分點的超額收益，是2020年3月以來最高水平——當年疫情衝擊全球，投資者爭相尋找避險資產。這一漲幅背後，是面對融資成本走高、AI交易行情遭遇質疑，疊加法國、西班牙等國政局動盪，歐洲投資者正在主動降低風險敞口。指數的上漲主要由三大權重股拉動：藥企羅氏控股10月上漲12%，諾華上漲6.7%；全球食品巨頭雀巢上漲10%。本月瑞士指數的全部漲幅，都來自這三隻老牌防禦龍頭。EFG資產管理瑞士股票主管達米安・伯克哈特表示：“投資者看好瑞士個股的防禦屬性，正在主動尋找擁擠的AI交易之外的替代方向。此前醫藥板塊的資金被抽走去追AI，現在這個趨勢正在反轉。”美銀最新基金經理調查同樣顯示，市場對瑞士股票的偏好顯著升溫，淨16%的受訪基金經理將瑞士選為最看好的歐洲股市。

### 2026-10-24 15:01:12  #公司

【大同證券景劍文：經濟韌性與盈利改善為A股中長期發展提供支撐】近日，大同證券宏觀策略分析師景劍文在接受中國證券報·中證金牛座記者採訪時表示，中國經濟展現出較強韌性。中長期看，A股市場仍有回暖基礎，經濟韌性、估值比較優勢和優質上市公司的成長潛力，是當前市場投資價值的重要體現。“結構上，科技板塊依然展現出了一定的亮眼表現。”景劍文表示，部分半導體、通訊領域龍頭公司延續了此前的亮眼表現，業績增幅依然較高，淨利潤實現同比、環比雙增，且業績兌現度較高，具備較強的業績支撐。談及行業估值，景劍文認為，多數行業估值並未明顯上移，上一輪行情更多由科技板塊業績驅動，其他板塊漲幅相對偏低。科技板塊近期調整，主要與市場對估值過高以及後續可能出現泡沫化的擔憂有關。在經歷一段時間的回落後，科技板塊在未出現大範圍業績回落的情況下，估值已較前期高位有所回落。對於當前A股市場，景劍文表示，短期波動受到情緒因素影響，投資者對利空訊息的敏感度上升，放大了部分利空因素的影響。但與此同時，諸多積極因素正在積聚，監管密集發聲，三季報披露階段也頻頻出現業績亮眼的公司，有助於緩解市場的擔憂情緒。“中長期看，市場仍有回暖基礎。”景劍文認為，國內經濟表現穩定，經濟韌效能夠為市場中長期穩定發展提供有力支撐。從結構上看，科技板塊雖經歷較大回撥，但估值有所回落，全球科技競賽持續，AI應用推動經營閉環逐步穩定，中長期發展趨勢依然向好。（中證金牛座）

【Local opposition rises in US; S&P warns of higher data‑center credit risk】S&P Global Ratings warned that construction suspensions and project cancellations driven by local public opposition are increasing credit risk for US data centers. Grid capacity limits, environmental disputes and zoning changes have delayed projects; S&P tracked 146 delayed or cancelled data‑center projects in the US, roughly three‑quarters linked to local opposition. The report highlights pauses in New York, Texas, Pennsylvania, Illinois and Arizona and notes 40 states have enacted local restrictions. Public concerns focus on energy costs, water stress, air quality and noise. S&P said current rating impacts remain limited but warned that sustained suspensions, slower approvals or policy reversals that cause significant delays could force developers to grant rent abatements or face early lease terminations, which would impair project creditworthiness and raise downgrade risk.

【信通院報告：大模型價格回升明顯，詞元價格較去年下半年翻倍，日均消耗量增長3倍】隨著大模型技術迅速迭代，詞元（Token）服務已成為技術走向應用的關鍵節點。10月23日，中國資訊通訊研究院人工智慧研究所、人工智慧大模型及軟硬體評測工業和資訊化部重點實驗室與中國人工智慧產業發展聯盟聯合釋出《大模型詞元服務效能監測報告（2026年）》（下稱報告）。報告顯示，詞元服務呈現量價齊升態勢，其中國內的詞元價格較去年下半年翻倍，但量的增長達到3倍。2026年6月，國內詞元日均消耗量約500萬億，是2025年12月的近4倍。越來越多模型即服務（MaaS）平臺廠商透過公有云API方式供給大模型詞元服務，而使用者對詞元服務效能缺乏直觀判斷依據、面臨選型困難，是該報告開展的背景。經歷過去兩年價格戰後，2026年國內外模型服務價格明顯上漲：歸一化（注：把各家不同的計價方式，折算到同一個統一口徑上，讓價格可以橫向比較）均價從2025年下半年的6.8元漲至今年的13.46元，漲幅接近一倍；但國外價格仍顯著高於國內，2026年以來發布的典型模型平均歸一價格約為117.8元。（澎湃）

【標普調整新規則，成長股與價值股分類將迎來重整】標普道瓊斯指數公司調整了股票劃分為成長股和價值股的規則，相關指數成分股或將迎來大幅調整。根據該公司週五釋出的宣告，新規則將把無形資產、預期盈利和自由現金流納入評估因素，以判斷一家公司的股價是否相對低估。同時，衡量公司成長潛力的指標將不再包括過去12個月的股價動能。此次調整是在近期徵求意見後作出的，將於12月21日市場開盤前的年度指數重組中正式生效。新規可能迫使追蹤相關指數的基金調整持倉，以匹配新的成分股名單。行業研究分析師詹姆斯·塞法特估計，至少有3750億美元的被動型基金資金與受影響的指數掛鉤。標普此次調整源於市場長期以來對傳統價值股估值指標是否仍然有效的爭論。研究人員和基金經理認為，價值股長期表現低迷，部分原因在於現有衡量標準存在缺陷，尤其是市淨率。

### 2026-10-24 11:34:58  #MKT News #IVW #Impact bearish #IVE #Impact bullish #SPYG

【S&P revises growth/value classification; index constituents set for overhaul】S&P Dow Jones Indices said it has revised rules for classifying stocks as growth or value, adding intangible assets, expected earnings and free cash flow to valuation assessments and removing 12‑month price momentum from growth metrics. The changes, adopted after a recent consultation, will take effect at the annual index reconstitution before market open on Dec. 21. The reclassification could force index-tracking funds to rebalance to match new constituents; analyst James Seifert estimates at least $375bn of passive assets are linked to affected indices. S&P said the move responds to long-running questions over the usefulness of traditional value measures; researchers and managers have pointed to value’s long-term underperformance and flaws in metrics such as price-to-book.

【Cui Dongshu: China power and other battery output 237 GWh in September, up 57% YoY】CPCA chair Cui Dongshu said China’s combined power and other battery production was 237 GWh in September, up 57% YoY, with Jan–Sep output at 1,833 GWh, up 63% YoY. He said battery growth has cooled from above 60% this year as power-battery demand weakened and export tax rebate adjustments have reduced demand. In Sep 2026 lithium battery vehicle fitment was 87 GWh, up 15% YoY; Jan–Sep 2026 fitment was 576 GWh, up 17% YoY. The share of production fitted to vehicles fell to 44% in 2025 and to 37% in Sep 2026; in Sep 2026 NMC (ternary) fitment rate was 35% and LFP fitment rate 37%. Domestic lithium-battery vehicle fitments in Sep 2026 were 1.26 mln units, down 12% YoY — BEV passenger cars 810k (-6%), PHEV passenger cars 350k (-29%), BEV trucks 82k (+22%). Cui said the production figures show pronounced divergence across segments.

【英國承諾出資1億英鎊設立生物安全實驗室，OpenAI、Anthropic 參與合作】英國宣佈出資 1 億英鎊（摺合 1.34 億美元）建設 AI 生物安全實驗室，由 OpenAI、Anthropic 以及谷歌 DeepMind 提供支援。這是一攬子舉措中的一項，旨在提升英國政府認知和應對人工智慧相關風險的能力。根據英國政府宣告，該實驗室將藉助人工智慧識別、監測生物威脅；與此同時，OpenAI 與 Anthropic 將提供最高 2 億英鎊額度的 AI 模型算力。實驗室還將與谷歌 DeepMind 以及生命科學企業 Basecamp Research 開展合作。政府另外撥款 6500 萬英鎊，用於研發病原體應對手段。英國 AI 大臣卡尼什卡・納拉揚表示：“隨著人工智慧能力持續增強，英國必須具備保護國民、自主塑造未來的能力。這意味著我們要走在網路攻擊、生物風險等新興威脅前面，同時更多掌握國家安全所依賴的關鍵技術。”本次公告同時包含追加撥款：向 AI 安全研究所增投 1.2 億英鎊。該機構是資金規模最高的政府機構，核心工作是持續評估前沿 AI 模型；另外撥付 2300 萬英鎊，用於籌建 AI 經濟研究所，分析人工智慧帶來的經濟影響。

【UK to spend £600m on AI safety to counter emerging biological threats】The UK will invest about £600m (about $800m) in AI safety targeting biological, health and cyber threats, the government said. Planned allocations include £100m to build an AI biosecurity laboratory, £65m for pathogen-response R&D, £120m for the existing AI Safety Institute, £130m to strengthen cyber defenses for critical infrastructure and the wider economy, and £60m to support AI-driven robotics, automation and manufacturing firms, alongside funding for AI research, chip design, medical policy innovation and local AI projects. ### 2026-10-24 05:49:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Credit Rating #Importance 0

France Avoids Moody’s Downgrade — Moody’s maintained France’s long-term foreign-currency sovereign credit rating at Aa3, defying expectations of a downgrade, but kept the outlook negative. The agency cited slower growth and a higher deficit, which have pushed the country’s debt burden above previous projections, as well as rising bond yields and social protests that could hinder fiscal consolidation. French yields have risen more than those of any other developed economy this month, reaching a 24-year high. France’s deficit is projected to reach 5.4% of GDP this year, as political gridlock, sluggish growth and rising interest costs undermine efforts to rein in public debt. Moody’s expects a deficit-reducing 2027 budget to be adopted despite the challenging political and social environment. The rating remains one notch above those of S&P and Fitch, which downgraded France during a budget dispute in autumn 2025. Last month, Scope Ratings also downgraded France, while Morningstar DBRS revised its outlook to negative.

### 2026-10-24 05:36:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Mexico #Currency #Importance 1

Mexican Peso Hits Near One-Year High — The Mexican peso strengthened to around 18.64 per US dollar in October, reaching its highest level in nearly a year. The currency appreciated following strong economic activity data, with the Global Indicator of Economic Activity rising 3.5% year over year through August, its strongest performance since September 2023. The exchange rate was also supported by expectations that Mexico and the US could reach a trade agreement, following comments by President Claudia Sheinbaum and US President Donald Trump indicating that the two countries were close to a deal. Meanwhile, annual mid-month inflation rose to 3.62% in October from 3.42% in September, slightly above forecasts of 3.60%. Persistent inflationary pressures could encourage a more hawkish stance by Banxico, while markets largely expect the US Federal Reserve to hold rates at its next meeting. A wider US-Mexico interest rate differential would strengthen the peso’s yield advantage and potentially encourage carry-trade inflows.

Rising oil prices have added to upward pressure on energy costs amid the escalation of the conflict in the Middle East. Stronger retail activity could add to inflation concerns and reinforce expectations for a tighter monetary policy stance.

Paraguay Producer Prices Fall for Eighth Consecutive Month — Paraguay’s Producer Price Index fell 0.8% year-on-year in September 2026, marking its eighth consecutive annual decrease. The decline was mainly driven by negative contributions from domestic agriculture and horticultural products and live animals and animal products, as well as imported metallic products, machinery and equipment, and food, beverages and tobacco, textiles, and leather products. Meanwhile, domestic non-metallic transportable goods and other manufactured products, along with imported non-metallic transportable goods, made positive contributions, helping to mitigate the overall decline. By product grouping, both domestic and imported products contributed negatively to the annual result.

Ibovespa Closes Higher Ahead of Presidential Runoff — The Ibovespa rose 0.5% to close at 205,083 on Friday in the final session before Brazil’s presidential runoff, recovering from earlier losses. A Datafolha poll showed President Lula and Flávio Bolsonaro tied at 50% each of valid votes. Earlier polls indicated a statistical tie. Some market participants view a potential Flávio Bolsonaro administration as more likely to rein in public spending, which they consider essential to lowering interest rates. However, neither the candidate nor his economic advisers have outlined concrete fiscal adjustments. The Ibovespa rallied to record highs after Flávio Bolsonaro finished ahead of Lula in the first round. Meanwhile, mid-month inflation came in below expectations, supporting equities. Banking stocks advanced, with BB gaining 3.3%. Bradesco added 0.7% following third-quarter earnings in line with market expectations and the announcement of a share buyback program. Petrobras rose 1.7% as oil prices rose. For the week, the Ibovespa fell 1.0%.

### 2026-10-24 04:36:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Paraguay #Interest Rate #Importance 0

Paraguay Central Bank Holds Policy Rate at 5.50% — Paraguay’s central bank kept its policy rate unchanged at 5.50% at it's October 2026 meeting. The decision came as economic activity remained strong, with the central bank revising its 2026 GDP growth forecast upward from 4.5% to 5.0%, supported by services and utilities. Annual inflation stood at 1.7% in September, while core inflation excluding food and energy reached 1.5%. The central bank lowered its year-end inflation forecast from 3.3% to 3.0%, reflecting a more moderate path for non-energy goods prices, and cut its core inflation projection from 2.8% to 2.2%. Inflation expectations remained anchored at 3.5%. Meanwhile, external risks persisted amid elevated oil prices and geopolitical tensions in the Middle East, alongside expectations of higher US interest rates through the remainder of 2026 and into 2027. The central bank reaffirmed its commitment to price stability and its 3.5% inflation target.

Uruguay Unemployment Rate Edged Lower in September — Uruguay’s unemployment rate inched down to 7.1% in September 2026 from 7.2% in August. By region, unemployment was equal in Montevideo and the interior, at 7.1%. The gender gap persisted, with female unemployment at 8.6%, compared with 5.8% for men. Unemployment varied significantly by age, reaching 21.6% among young people aged 14 to 24, the group facing the greatest difficulties entering the labor market. Regarding employment quality, underemployment stood at 8.8%, while the share of workers not registered with social security reached 21%. Both indicators remained broadly stable month-over-month.

【中方：國際原子能機構應停止審議伊核問題】中國常駐聯合國副代表孫磊23日在聯大審議“國際原子能機構年度報告”時發言，指出國際原子能機構應停止審議伊朗核問題。孫磊說，國際原子能機構應秉持客觀公正立場，嚴格根據授權履行職責，為有關各方相向而行、恢復對話談判提供協助。伊核問題最終要透過外交途徑妥善解決，訴諸武力、制裁施壓只會激化矛盾。伊朗作為《不擴散核武器條約》無核武器締約國，應履行不發展核武器承諾。伊朗和平利用核能的合法權利應得到尊重。他說，核可伊朗核問題全面協議的安理會第2231號決議已到期終止，安理會及國際原子能機構應停止審議伊核問題，國際原子能機構總幹事應停止提交有關報告。孫磊指出，美英澳核潛艇合作違反《不擴散核武器條約》宗旨和目標，存在嚴重核擴散風險，對條約和國際原子能機構保障監督體系構成嚴峻挑戰。國際社會已充分認識到有關問題的複雜性與敏感性，應在平等和相互尊重基礎上，透過公開、透明、包容、可持續的政府間程序，尋求各方均可接受的解決方案。（新華社）

### 2026-10-24 01:10:27  #MKT News #Brent #Impact bullish #XAUUSD #DXY

【Report: Iran has detailed plan to strike Gulf undersea communications cables】Reuters: Two senior Iranian security officials said Iran has drawn up detailed plans to strike undersea communications cables in the Gulf if the US or Israel attacks, and could impede repairs to prolong outages. One official warned that if Iran is attacked "there will be no red lines", with submarine cables and military bases potentially at risk. The IRGC deputy for political affairs previously threatened strikes on seven main internet cables under the Strait of Hormuz, and a senior security official warned US-linked data centers and AI facilities in the Gulf could be targeted. Damage to cables could disrupt regional internet access, financial transactions, e‑commerce and cloud services. TeleGeography head of research Alan Mauldin said the Strait of Hormuz carries under 1% of global international bandwidth, so global impact is likely limited, but regional outages and wartime repair difficulties are material. The Red Sea is also a potential risk area, though Iran has not publicly said it would target cables there.

### 2026-10-23 23:56:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

NatGas Prices Fall to Almost 6-Month Low — US natural gas prices fell more than 5% on Friday to nearly $2.65 per MMBtu, approaching their lowest level since late April, as forecasts for milder weather weighed on expectations for heating demand. Above-average temperatures are expected across the Midwest and East through November 5, according to Commodity Weather Group, likely curbing gas consumption. The price decline came despite a smaller-than-usual storage build, strong LNG feedgas demand, and lower production. EIA data showed that US natural gas inventories rose by 77 Bcf in the week ended October 16, below both the 86 Bcf added during the same week last year and the five-year average increase of 87 Bcf. Meanwhile, average feedgas flows to the nine major US LNG export terminals climbed to 18.2 Bcf/d in October, up from 17.9 Bcf/d in September. On the supply side, production across the Lower 48 states averaged 111.4 Bcf/d this month, down from record highs of 113.3 Bcf/d in August and September.

【澳門特區政府舉行政府引導基金設立檔案簽署儀式】澳門特區政府23日舉行政府引導基金設立檔案簽署儀式，標誌基金正式運作。 儀式在特區政府總部舉行，由澳門特區行政長官岑浩輝、澳門中聯辦主任鄭新聰、特區政府經濟財政司司長吳惠嫻出席見證，澳門投資管理有限公司董事長謝永強與6家綜合旅遊休閒企業代表進行簽署。 據介紹，政府引導基金首期規模達200億澳門元，分別由特區政府以財政儲備歷年滾存收益出資110億澳門元，以及由6家綜合旅遊休閒企業作為首批社會資本合共出資90億澳門元組成。 基金按第11/2025號法律《投資基金法》及第26/2026號行政法規《政府引導基金管理制度》設立及運作，由澳門投資管理有限公司負責管理。基金貫徹長期主義及耐心資本的理念，採用“引導基金—母基金—子基金”三層架構，母基金及子基金由公開遴選的專業基金經理按市場化方式管理。 基金重點投向新興產業、產業轉型升級、科技成果轉化及初創科技企業等範疇，支援澳門與橫琴粵澳深度合作區、粵港澳大灣區其他城市聯動發展。(新華社)

### 2026-10-23 22:46:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Gold Slides Below $4,000 — Gold prices fell below $4,000 an ounce on Friday, hitting their lowest level since mid-July and putting the precious metal down almost 4% for the week, as rising oil prices strengthened expectations that US interest rates will remain higher for longer. Brent crude surged to a four-year high after Houthi attacks in Saudi Arabia heightened concerns over further energy supply disruptions. Meanwhile, recent US data showed consumer and producer inflation rising more than expected, largely driven by higher energy costs, reinforcing concerns over persistent inflationary pressures. Despite this, Federal Reserve officials are widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged at their October 27–28 meeting. Markets currently price in just a 10% chance of a rate hike next week, compared with odds of around 80% for December, according to CME’s FedWatch Tool. Investors now await US PCE inflation data due October 29 for further clues on progress towards the Fed’s 2% inflation target.

【王毅會見聯合國難民事務高階專員薩利赫】2026年10月23日，中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅在河北雄安新區會見來華出席2026全球治理論壇（雄安）的聯合國難民事務高階專員薩利赫。王毅表示，難民問題和全球治理密切相關。習近平主席提出全球治理倡議，對國際社會攜手應對難民挑戰具有指導意義。中國高度重視聯合國難民署的地位和作用，願同難民署更密切合作。中方認為，消除難民危機，和平是首要。中國盡己所能推動解決全球熱點糾紛，積極投入聯合國維和行動。改善難民處境，發展是基礎。中國為發展中國家特別是最不發達國家提供大量人道援助和支援，為從根源上解決難民問題創造條件。完善難民治理，國際合作是關鍵。中方歡迎並支援難民署在全球難民治理中發揮引領作用，願同難民署共同探討解決主要難民來源國問題的有效途徑。(外交部網站)

【美國企業活動保持擴張 價格創2022年以來最大漲幅】美國10月企業活動增速略有放緩，但仍接近多年高位，強勁需求推動新訂單、就業和價格上升。週五公佈的資料顯示，標普全球10月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)初值下降0.8點至57.6。這是2022年以來的第二高水平。指數高於50代表擴張。銷售價格指標以逾四年來最快速度上漲，反映能源、運輸和原材料成本大幅上升。標普全球市場財智的首席商業經濟學家Chris Williamson在宣告中表示：“成本壓力仍是主要擔憂，尤其是油價再度飆升的影響傳導至廣泛的商品和服務領域。”供應瓶頸也在惡化。供應商交貨時間出現2022年以來最大幅度的延長，標普全球指出，原因包括材料和勞動力短缺、供應商超負荷運轉以及運輸延誤。即便如此，Williamson表示，如果不考慮疫情後經濟重啟時期，美國企業正經歷2015年以來最強勁的一段增長期。強勁的消費者支出、AI推動的投資以及國防支出今年都為美國企業提供了支撐。衡量需求的新訂單指標10月創下四年半來最快增速。

### 2026-10-23 22:06:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

US Consumer Sentiment Revised Higher but Remains Weak — The University of Michigan’s consumer sentiment index was revised higher to 48.0 in late October 2026, up from a preliminary estimate of 46.3, but remained at its lowest level in five months. Gains in sentiment among Republicans and Democrats were offset by declines among independents. Year-ahead business conditions improved by around 14%, although the index remained 16% below its level in October 2025. Meanwhile, the decline in the Current Conditions subindex was driven entirely by buying conditions for durable goods, which plunged 18% amid higher interest rates and growing concerns about affordability. Purchasing power continues to dominate consumer views of the economy, with sentiment now 10% below its year-ago level and 15% below February, just before the Iran conflict. Year-ahead inflation expectations edged up to 4.7% from 4.6% in September, while long-run inflation expectations also increased, rising to 3.5% from 3.4%.

TSX Falls as Oil Rally Fuels BoC Rate Hike Bets — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell more than 0.5% to hover near 35,500 on Friday as the oil rally continued to fuel inflation concerns, while technology stocks remained under pressure. Crude oil prices climbed amid escalating Houthi attacks on Saudi targets and reports of possible renewed US strikes against Iran. Canadian bond yields moved higher, with markets pricing in a Bank of Canada rate hike at its next meeting amid energy-driven inflationary pressures. Major Canadian banks posted losses, with RBC, TD Bank, BMO, Scotiabank and CIBC each down about 1%. Meanwhile, technology stocks remained under pressure as the rotation away from AI-related shares continued. Celestica extended its losses, falling more than 3.5% after plunging 12% in the previous session, while Shopify shed nearly 1%. Celestica is scheduled to report its third-quarter 2026 results on Monday. Elsewhere, gold prices fluctuated and silver fell, weighing on mining stocks. On the week, the TSX shed near 2%.

### 2026-10-23 21:59:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Services PMI #Importance 1

US Services Activity Growth Remains Robust — The S&P Global US Services PMI eased to 57.9 in October 2026 from 58.8 in September and marginally below market forecasts of 58, according to a flash estimate. The latest reading marked the seventh straight month of growth in business activity, with the pace of expansion remaining among the strongest since 2011, despite a slight moderation from September. New business growth accelerated to its fastest pace since March 2022, supported by stronger domestic demand, while export orders recorded their largest increase since July 2023. Employment growth moderated compared with September but was still the third largest for over four years. However, price pressures intensified, with services inflation reaching its highest level since August 2022. This reflected a further sharp increase in business costs, driven by rising fuels, alongside higher wages and rising utility and insurance expenses. Business output expectations for the 12 months ahead cooled down from September's one-year high.

【王毅談全球治理倡議的時代價值和中國擔當】據外交部網站訊息，2026年10月23日，中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅在2026全球治理論壇（雄安）開幕式上表示，一年前，在二戰後國際秩序面臨嚴峻挑戰，全球治理不進則退的重要關頭，習近平主席歷史性提出全球治理倡議，提供了彌補治理赤字、共創美好未來的時代方案。一年來，全球治理倡議的時代價值更加彰顯。倡議目前已得到160多個國家和國際組織的支援響應，60多國踴躍加入“全球治理之友小組”，倡議的核心要義已寫入二十國集團、上合組織、金磚國家等多邊會議檔案。國際社會認為，倡議在世界格局深刻調整之時捍衛多邊主義，在叢林法則捲土重來之際維護公平正義，為共謀發展、共享安全提供了清晰路徑，為動盪不安世界注入了寶貴的確定性。一年來，完善全球治理的中國貢獻更加突出。我們積極踐行中國特色熱點問題解決之道，習近平主席相繼提出維護和促進中東和平穩定的系列主張。我們堅持開放發展、推動共同發展，宣佈在世貿組織當前和未來談判中不尋求新的特殊和差別待遇，對非洲和最不發達建交國全面實施零關稅。我們積極提供新型公共產品，國際調解院已成功解決國際爭議案件，發起成立世界人工智慧合作組織，旗幟鮮明推動人工智慧向上向善、造福人類。一年來，對中國發揮更大作用的國際期待更加熱切。國際社會有識之士認為，習近平主席提出的全球治理倡議是對現有國際體系的修復與完善，是為建設更加公平、包容、可持續的世界貢獻的中國智慧。過去一年，國際民意對中國的好感度大幅提升，其中包含了對中國作為全球穩定與進步力量的高度認可，對中國提出並踐行全球治理倡議的充分肯定。

Ibovespa Fluctuates Ahead of Presidential Runoff — The Ibovespa fluctuated around the 204,000 mark on Friday in the final session before Brazil’s presidential runoff. A Datafolha poll showed Senator Flávio Bolsonaro and President Lula tied at 50% each of valid votes. Earlier polls also indicated a statistical tie, with Bolsonaro’s lead narrowing compared with previous surveys. Some market participants believe a Flávio Bolsonaro administration would be more likely to rein in public spending, although neither the candidate nor his economic advisers have outlined concrete fiscal adjustments. Financial stocks mostly traded lower, with Itaú edging down and Banco do Brasil and Itaúsa losing more than 0.5%. Bradesco fell nearly 2.5% despite reporting third-quarter earnings in line with market expectations and announcing a share buyback program. In contrast, Petrobras rose nearly 2% as oil prices climbed following fresh attacks in the Middle East. Vale gained nearly 1% as iron ore prices rebounded. On the week, the Ibovespa shed over 0.5%.

### 2026-10-23 21:52:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Composite PMI #Importance 2

US Business Activity Growth Holds Close to 5-Year High — The US Composite PMI eased to 57.6 in October 2026 from 58.4 in September, a two-month low, but remained firmly in expansion territory and signalled the second-strongest increase in business activity since March 2022. Growth in new orders strengthened to its highest level in more than four-and-a-half years, while export sales recorded their largest gain since May 2022. Employment also remained strong, rising at its fastest pace since June 2022 as companies increased staffing to meet demand and address growing backlogs. However, mounting cost pressures drove selling-price inflation to its highest level since August 2022. Input costs rose at their fastest pace since November 2022, excluding September’s increase. Meanwhile, business confidence for the year ahead weakened slightly but remained the second-highest reading recorded this year.

US Manufacturing Conditions Strongest Since 2022 — The S&P Global US Manufacturing PMI edged up to 56 in October 2026 from 55.9 in September, beating forecasts of 55.5, preliminary estimates showed. The reading pointed to the strongest improvement in operating conditions in manufacturing since May 2022. Although production and new orders growth rates slowed, both remained elevated to register sustained strong expansions. Suppliers’ delivery times meanwhile lengthened to the greatest degree since July 2022.

Baltic Dry Index Snaps 4-Day Winning Run — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, snapped its four-day winning streak on Friday, falling by 1.2% to 2,660 points. The panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, dropped roughly 2% to a fresh low since August 24 at 2,109 points. Meanwhile, the capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, also halted its four-day winning run, decreasing by 1.5% to 3,791 points. Among smaller vessels, the supramax index went down by 0.2% to 1,887 points. For the week, the benchmark index advanced about 5.9%, notching its second consecutive weekly gain, mainly supported by the bigger-size segment.

### 2026-10-23 21:15:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Belgium #Business Confidence #Importance 0

Belgium Business Pessimism Eases to Lowest Since January — Belgium’s business confidence indicator rose by 2.3 points to -9.8 in October 2026, the highest reading since January, compared to -12.1 in September and above market expectations of -11.8. Manufacturing sentiment became less pessimistic (-9.2 vs. -12.2), reflecting less negative assessments of total order books (-18.6 vs. -20.2) and employment expectations (-6.8 vs. -7.4), alongside a decline in stock levels (0.5 vs. 10.4). The confidence index also strengthened in the building industry (-8.3 vs -11.8), led by the trend in orders (0.5 vs -6.9) and the assessment of order books (-20.4 vs -24.5), as well as in business-related services (-12.6 vs -13.4), driven by activity expectations (-4.1 vs -7.4). In contrast, pessimism deepened in the trade sector (-13.2 vs -8.6), with employment expectations falling sharply (-13.0 vs -1.4).

【Canada retail rebound likely to continue in September】Preliminary Statistics Canada data show retail sales rose 0.7% YoY in September, with growth expected to slow from August's 1.2% increase that was driven by gains across all nine subsectors. 【加拿大零售額回升勢頭預計將在9月持續】加拿大零售支出預計將在9月份繼續保持增長，儘管增速可能較8月有所放緩。加拿大統計局的初步資料顯示，9月份零售銷售額同比增長0.7%。此前，受全部九個子行業增長的推動，8月份零售額增長了1.2%。8月份按銷量計算的銷售額也有所回升，增長了1.2%。鑑於9月與美國的貿易戰升級，經濟學家此前曾警告稱，8月份的反彈可能只是暫時的。加拿大經濟似乎已擺脫了第二季度因關稅引發的低迷，但7月和8月的GDP增速趨於平緩。零售資料是加拿大央行決策者在下週利率決議前，關於加拿大經濟狀況所能獲得的最後一條線索。過去一週，多位交易員和經濟學家改變了立場，現在押注加息。隔夜互換市場交易員將下週會議加息的機率推高至80%以上。

【央企資產總額突破95萬億元】2026國資國企高質量發展創新大會正在北京舉辦。今天會上釋出了《國資國企高質量發展觀察報告（2026）》，從實力躍升、結構最佳化、產業帶動等維度，系統評估國資國企改革發展成效。資料顯示，國資央企規模實力實現了歷史性躍升。中央企業資產總額從“十三五”末68.8萬億元增長至95萬億元，“十四五”累計實現利潤總額12.7萬億元，比“十三五”增長56.2%；全員勞動生產率提升至每人每年83.6萬元，年均增長7.1%。創新成為鮮明主線。中央企業研發投入累計超過5萬億元，一批大國重器、重大科技成果競相湧現；戰略性新興產業投資佔比提升至41.8%，佈局結構持續向新。功能擔當更加有力。中央企業保障國內約80%原油產量、70%天然氣產量、60%電力供應；年採購規模超過15萬億元，帶動上下游近200萬家企業協同發展；市值管理首次納入經營業績考核，國有資本的市場價值創造能力持續增強。從區域看，上海、廣州、雄安、東北等地形成差異化發展樣本；從全球看，我國國有企業在世界頭部企業中的分量持續提升。（央視新聞）

Canada Retail Sales Rise for 2nd Month — Retail sales in Canada likely increased 0.7% month-over-month in September 2026, extending a 1.2% rise in August, according to preliminary estimates. Meanwhile, data for August was revised down from a preliminary estimate of a 1.3% increase. In August, the largest gain in retail sales was observed at motor vehicle and parts dealers (+1.2%), led by new car dealers (1.5%). In August, gains at automotive parts, accessories and tire retailers (+1.4%) were offset by lower sales at other motor vehicle dealers (-1.4%) and used car dealers (-0.5%). Also, sales at gasoline stations and fuel vendors increased 1.7%, up for the first time in three months. In volume terms however, sales at gasoline stations and fuel vendors decreased 0.5%.

【國家防總、應急管理部針對江蘇啟動防汛防颱風四級應急響應】今年第30號颱風“薔琵”（超強颱風級）中心10月23日18時距離臺灣台北市東偏南方向約180公里，中心附近最大風力14級（45米每秒）。預計，“薔琵”將以每小時5－10公里速度向西偏北方向緩慢移動，逐漸向臺灣東北部靠近，移入東海南部後，將於25日前後沿浙江東部近海北上，之後轉向東北方向。受冷空氣和颱風共同影響，23日至26日，我國東南部海域及華東沿海有持續大風，江蘇、上海、浙江等地部分地區有大到暴雨。中央氣象臺23日18時釋出颱風藍色預警和暴雨藍色預警。根據《國家防汛抗旱應急預案》及有關規定，國家防總、應急管理部決定於23日19時針對江蘇啟動防汛防颱風四級應急響應。（央視新聞）

Chile’s Producer Inflation Renews 4-Month High — Chile’s producer inflation accelerated to 24.8% year-on-year in September 2026, up from 23.6% in August, reaching its highest level since April 2022. Price growth picked up across all sectors, led by mining, where inflation rose to 41.8% from 40.2%, driven by higher copper prices. Copper prices surged 43.5%, accelerating from a 41.3% increase in August. Manufacturing inflation also accelerated to 7.4% from 6.9%, while utility price growth edged up to 1.2% from 1.0%. On a monthly basis, producer prices rose 2.7%, following a 2.8% increase in August.

Mexican Economic Growth Beats Forecasts in August — Mexico’s economic activity rose 3.3% year over year in August 2026, easing slightly from 3.4% growth in July but exceeding forecasts of 3.1%. Tertiary activities led the expansion, growing 3.6%, supported by wholesale trade (12.1%), other services excluding government activities (7.3%) and information services in mass media (5.0%). Year to date, economic activity grew 1.7%.

【SAFE seeks comment on lowering category risk conversion factor to 0.5 for affiliate foreign borrowing】The State Administration of Foreign Exchange (SAFE) published a draft notice seeking comments on measures to further ease cross-border investment and financing facilitation. Under the draft’s macroprudential management framework, qualified foreign‑invested enterprises that borrow foreign debt from overseas affiliates would see their category risk conversion factor reduced from 1 to 0.5. 【幾內亞交通事故易發、高發，中使館提醒在幾中國公民和企業注意交通安全】今年以來，幾內亞發生多起涉中國公民交通事故，造成嚴重人員傷亡。幾內亞交通事故易發、高發，違章駕駛特別是酒駕醉駕極易釀成嚴重後果，造成惡劣影響。駐幾內亞大使館在此鄭重提醒全體在幾中國公民和企業，務必高度重視交通安全，嚴格遵守幾內亞當地法律法規及交通管理規定，牢固樹立安全行車意識。日常駕車出行自覺控制車速、文明駕駛、謹慎駕駛，堅決杜絕超速、闖紅燈、酒駕醉駕等違法行為。途經安檢點位時，請按指示提前減速，配合檢查，保持冷靜，避免與執法人員發生語言衝突，切勿採取強行衝卡闖關等極度危險行為，共同維護海外中國公民和企業安全和遵紀守法的良好形象。

【SAFE consults on draft: overseas-investment standby funds remittable up to $200,000/month, capped at 10% of registered ODI remittance quota】The State Administration of Foreign Exchange (SAFE) published a draft notice seeking comments on measures to further ease cross-border investment and financing. The draft permits domestic institutions to remit funds abroad as overseas-investment standby funds for small, routine operating expenses of overseas investees (rent, utilities, staff costs, office, transport, taxes, petty procurement). Banks may process remittances on presentation of a commitment letter specifying offshore use. Remittances are limited to the equivalent of $200,000 per month and cumulatively may not exceed 10% of the domestic institution’s registered outbound direct investment (ODI) remittance quota. Misuse of standby funds will be investigated and dealt with according to law.

Despite the modest rebound, cautious sentiment persisted as oil prices climbed further amid concerns over a potential escalation of hostilities in the Middle East, with the US midterm elections also less than two weeks away. Meanwhile, concerns over heavy AI-related spending continued to weigh on sentiment. The Federal Reserve is set to announce its monetary policy decision next week, with markets pricing in a roughly 91% probability of rates remaining unchanged. Meanwhile, the odds of a 25bps rate hike in December stood at around 76%.

### 2026-10-23 19:41:05  #宏觀

【李強主持召開國務院常務會議 研究統籌人口與經濟社會發展有關工作 聽取推進全國服務業大會部署落實情況彙報】國務院總理李強10月23日主持召開國務院常務會議，研究統籌人口與經濟社會發展有關工作，聽取推進全國服務業大會部署落實情況彙報。

會議指出，人口是經濟社會發展的基礎條件，要更好統籌人口與經濟社會、資源環境的關係，進一步完善相關政策，持續積累和釋放人口綜合紅利，以人口高質量發展更好支撐經濟社會高質量發展。要更加突出人的全面發展，提高人口科學文化、職業技能、身心健康等水平，加快完善全民終身學習機制，廣泛開展技能培訓，強化教育、培訓與產業發展協同聯動，促進高質量充分就業。要加強區域人口發展分類指導，引導城鄉人口有序流動，穩步推進基本公共服務均等化，根據人口分佈情況最佳化基礎設施佈局，推動公共服務資源與常住人口變化動態適配。要加快建設生育友好型社會，持續完善婚嫁、生育、養育、教育等政策，最佳化面向家庭的財稅、社保、住房等支援舉措，努力穩定新出生人口規模。

會議指出，要進一步發揮服務業對經濟發展的支撐作用，加力推進服務業擴能提質，把服務業潛力空間轉化為發展動能。要加快破解服務業發展的難點堵點，聚焦生產生活重點領域因業施策，加強融資、用地、人才、資料等支援，穩步推進公共事業領域價格改革，加大力度盤活用好閒置資源。要按照建設全國統一大市場的要求，持續破除市場準入障礙，清理民間投資隱性壁壘，加快構建與新業態發展相適應的監管方式。要充分調動各方面發展服務業的積極性，加強分類指導和督促協調，增強政策針對性有效性，支援有條件的地方先行先試，因地制宜探索服務業創新發展路徑。要鼓勵擴大服務業投資，釋放服務消費潛力，培育龍頭企業和特色品牌。要進一步健全適應服務業發展的統計監測體系，強化上下聯動、齊抓共管的工作格局。

會議還研究了其他事項。（新華社）

【方正證券燕翔：五大積極因素支撐A股長期向好】近期A股震盪加劇，方正證券首席經濟學家燕翔最新觀點認為，短期波動未改變市場長期向好趨勢，宏觀經濟向新向優、企業盈利回升、估值處於底部區域、股東回報增強、制度改革持續推進五大因素，決定中國資本市場配置價值將持續凸顯。燕翔分析，前三季度GDP超107萬億元、同比增長4.6%，新動能對經濟增長貢獻率保持在40%以上，經濟韌性和新質生產力為資本市場提供堅實基本盤。上市公司半年報顯示，全市場營收超37萬億元、同比增長7.6%，歸母淨利潤約3.6萬億元、同比增長近20%，二季度淨利潤增速接近30%，盈利回升態勢明顯。估值方面，截至10月23日，滬深300市盈率TTM降至12.15倍，處於近10年49%分位；上證指數為13.16倍、61%分位；創業板指降至27.3倍，處於近10年15%以下分位。燕翔認為，A股整體估值已進入合理甚至偏低區間，進一步下行空間相對有限。股東回報方面，上市公司年內分紅回購規模達2.1萬億元，是同期IPO融資和再融資的2.7倍。制度改革方面，證監會提出維護市場穩定、深化包容性改革、培育健康市場生態、擴大高水平開放等舉措。燕翔表示，制度紅利的持續釋放，將不斷增強中國股市吸引力與配置價值。

【法國國債本週經歷歷史性拋售 市場關注穆迪是否會下調該國評級】法國債券市場本週經歷了歷史性動盪。隨著政治局勢不安可能進一步加劇市場對該國公共財政的擔憂，一項備受關注的風險指標正接近有記錄以來最大升幅。資料顯示，本週迄今，投資者持有法國10年期國債而非德國同期限國債所要求的風險溢價已上升33個基點，為1990年有記錄以來最大升幅。法國10年期國債收益率週五企穩在5%左右，CAC 40指數則下跌。投資者擔心，在債務負擔沉重、經濟增長乏力，以及公眾不滿演變成混亂街頭示威的背景下，法國正面臨危險的前景。市場對本週末再度爆發抗議活動的擔憂，也令企業高管在週末前保持警惕。更廣泛而言，隨著油價升至每桶100美元上方，加之市場對AI盈利能力感到不安，全球風險資產均遭到拋售。

### 2026-10-23 19:25:54  #其他

【華福證券陳興：建議投資者規避短期情緒干擾，避免恐慌式離場】華福證券宏觀首席分析師陳興接受證券時報記者採訪時表示，此前市場調整，主要受美債收益率高位執行、海外AI融資預期走弱帶來的外部估值壓制影響，國內基本面並未出現惡化。9月下旬指數加速回撥後，市場情緒與投資者倉位已回落至低位。伴隨三季報披露進入尾聲，盈利修復拐點有望進一步確認，疊加增量資金持續進場，市場或將逐步消化外部擾動，迴歸基本面定價主線。陳興建議投資者規避短期情緒干擾，避免恐慌式離場，聚焦業績與估值匹配度較好的優質標的，保持理性、耐心佈局。陳興表示，從宏觀基本面看，我國經濟呈現“總量穩、動能新、邊際改善”的積極特徵。結構上，第三產業增加值同比增長5.2%，成為穩增長的核心支撐，“新興領域良性迴圈加快形成”，高技術產業與新質生產力對增長的貢獻持續提升。整體來看，三季度經濟環比提速、景氣邊際回升，一定程度上打破了前期市場對於基本面偏弱的悲觀預期，也為後續企業盈利兌現、市場估值修復奠定了基本面基礎。

### 2026-10-23 19:20:31  #其他

【DeepSeek完成約1000億元新融資 產業資本攜資源密集入局】記者從多個一級投資人處確認，DeepSeek近日已完成約1000億元人民幣（約150億美元）融資，重新整理中國AI行業單輪融資紀錄。據悉，本輪融資目標由前期的800億元上調至1000億元。老股東寧德時代、騰訊控股作為主要投資方參與本輪融資，IDG資本、礪思資本及拾象資本繼續跟投。新晉投資方多為產業資本，包括聯想系君聯資本、兆易創新系石溪資本、吉利汽車產業基金、中芯聚源，以及後期加入的橫店資本等。針對本輪融資的產業邏輯，一名投資人士向證券時報記者表示，當前大模型的發展本質上是工程問題，底層架構已無明顯秘密，核心壁壘在於訓練資料集與算力資源。因此，DeepSeek在此次融資中對投資方提出了明確的資源協同要求，要求所有意向投資方必須說明能夠為DeepSeek提供何種資料支援與業務協同。在此導向下，具備深厚產業資源與協同能力的機構獲得了更多份額，例如橫店資本等獲得了較大額度的投資份額。（證券時報）

【重慶市璧山區人大常委會原黨組副書記朱朝陽被“雙開”】中央紀委國家監委網站10月23日通報，據重慶市紀委監委訊息：經重慶市委批准，重慶市紀委監委對璧山區人大常委會原黨組副書記、副主任朱朝陽嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，朱朝陽喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，官迷心竅、利令智昏，結交政治騙子，對抗組織審查，搞迷信活動；無視中央八項規定精神，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請和旅遊安排；違反組織原則，不按規定報告個人有關事項，在職工錄用、工作調動中為他人謀利並收受財物；廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮品禮金，違規從事營利活動，違規借用管理和服務物件錢款車輛；違規干預和插手司法活動；私慾膨脹，利用職務便利為他人在土地出讓、工程承攬、企業經營等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。朱朝陽嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經重慶市紀委常委會會議研究並報重慶市委批准，決定給予朱朝陽開除黨籍處分；由重慶市監委給予其開除公職處分；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

### 2026-10-23 18:55:50  #國際

【專家：伊朗為與美談判留有空間】伊朗極少公佈最高領袖穆傑塔巴·哈梅內伊線下會面的訊息，他上一次被報道的面對面會見，還是今年8月與伊朗總統佩澤希齊揚會晤，隨後伊朗國內啟動一輪人事調整。如今，在美以醞釀新一輪打擊的關鍵節點，穆傑塔巴10月21日與伊朗議長、首席談判代表卡利巴夫的會見對外公開，傳遞出哪些關鍵訊號？中國現代國際關係研究院中東研究所副所長秦天分析指出，穆傑塔巴相關活動的公佈往往是在一些重要時機，此次會見卡利巴夫是要處理三方面重要問題：其一，是備戰打仗的問題，以應對美國中期選舉後，美以醞釀的新軍事進攻；其二，此次會面也是伊朗最高領袖鞏固個人權威的一個步驟，顯示其仍在掌控局面，在關鍵重大問題上決定國家方向；其三，也是為與美國談判留有靈活空間——美以伊衝突爆發以來，卡利巴夫在伊朗政壇上代表了與美國談判的立場。穆傑塔巴選擇與其會見，而非伊朗總統或革命衛隊領導人，可能是有意在緊張局勢下，探索談判的可能性。 （CCTV國際時訊）

【俄羅斯央行連續第二次維持利率不變，烏克蘭打擊能源設施帶來經濟壓力】俄羅斯央行週五宣佈維持基準利率14%不變，這是連續第二個議息會議按兵不動，符合彭博調查11位經濟學家的預期，僅一位經濟學家預計降息25個基點。俄羅斯央行在宣告中表示：“當前物價壓力仍處於高位。”中期來看，推升通脹的風險佔據主導；貨幣政策決議將“依據物價漲幅、通脹預期變化，以及對內外部環境風險的研判”做出。央行行長埃爾薇拉·納比烏林娜將於莫斯科時間15:00召開新聞釋出會。烏克蘭針對煉油設施的打擊行動，令原油加工率跌至多年低位，並引發燃料短缺，進一步加劇通脹壓力，通脹水平已經超出央行最新年末預測。俄央行原本預計煉油廠將在下半年逐步恢復生產，但持續襲擊造成新的損毀，部分剛復產的設施也受到波及。

### 2026-10-23 18:47:45  #央行 #公司 #市場

【大同證券宏觀策略分析師景劍文：受情緒影響產生的非理性下跌無需過於恐慌，科技板塊中長期發展趨勢依然向好】10月23日，大同證券宏觀策略分析師景劍文表示，當前市場短期波動主要受恐慌情緒主導，投資者對利空訊息的敏感度上升，放大了部分利空因素的影響，導致出現一定程度的非理性下跌。然而，理性審視下，這些利空因素進一步惡化的可能性相對偏低，情緒面衝擊更多是階段性的。與此同時，諸多利好因素正在疊加積聚，金融街論壇召開，央行、金融監管總局、證監會密集發聲，多項穩定市場舉措有望落地；市場層面亦有增量資金進場，多隻ETF現大額資金流入；三季報也有科技板塊公司展現亮眼業績。多重利好有助於緩解近期市場持續下挫的恐慌情緒。景劍文認為，中長期看，市場仍有回暖基礎。宏觀層面，全球環境未顯著惡化，國內經濟資料表現穩定，經濟韌性為市場中長期穩定發展提供支撐。科技板塊雖經歷較大回撥，但估值顯著回落、風險出清程度較高，全球科技競賽持續、大廠資本開支未見縮減，AI應用推動經營閉環逐步穩定，中長期發展趨勢依然向好。他表示，短期受情緒影響產生的非理性下跌無需過於恐慌，反而科技板塊在持續回落、出清風險後，有望重回主線帶動市場上行，放眼長期，或可逢低佈局科技相關板塊。

### 2026-10-23 18:46:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Russia #Currency #Importance 0

Russian Ruble Hovers at 3-Month High — The Russian ruble hovered around 81 per US dollar on Friday, reaching its strongest level in nearly three months after the Bank of Russia kept its key interest rate unchanged at 14%, in line with market expectations. The central bank maintained a cautious stance as persistent inflationary pressures and elevated expectations continue to complicate its efforts to bring inflation back to the 4% target. Annual inflation stood at an estimated 6.8% as of October 19, while the bank expects it to reach 7.1–7.6% by year-end. Ukrainian attacks on Russian energy infrastructure have disrupted refinery operations, contributed to fuel shortages and increased transportation costs, adding to price pressures. The central bank also warned that capacity constraints and wage growth exceeding productivity gains pose further inflation risks. Policymakers forecast an average key rate of 12.5–13.5% in 2027 and expect inflation to return to target that year.

【MIIT holds Hangzhou meeting to accelerate IT-driven advanced manufacturing】Oct 22 — China’s Ministry of Industry and Information Technology said at a Hangzhou meeting it will accelerate information technology to drive high‑quality development of advanced manufacturing. ### 2026-10-23 18:40:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Finishes Week Lower — India’s BSE Sensex closed about 0.2% down at an over one-week low of 73,124 on Friday, marking the fourth straight session of losses and posting a 2.1% weekly drop. Market sentiment continued to be dominated by concerns over rising oil prices, geopolitical tensions and uncertainty regarding future developments. High oil prices pose risks to India’s import bill, inflation outlook, and corporate margins. Adding to the pressure, foreign institutional investors sold Indian equities worth 3.46 billion rupees on October 22. Meanwhile, flash PMI data showed Indian private sector growth accelerated to a four-month high in October. Selling remained widespread across sectors, with metal and consumer durables stocks among the biggest laggards. Tata Steel led the losses, falling 3.2%, followed by Adani Ports, which declined 3.03%. Meanwhile, Eternal recorded the strongest performance, gaining roughly 4%. Larsen & Toubro advanced 1.3%, Reliance Industries rose 1.2%, and TCS increased by 1%.

### 2026-10-23 18:34:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Russia #Interest Rate #Importance 2

Russia Holds Interest Rate as Expected — The Bank of Russia kept its key interest rate unchanged at 14% in October, in line with market expectations, as inflationary pressures and elevated expectations continued to hinder disinflation. Ukrainian strikes on Russian energy infrastructure disrupted refinery operations, pushed crude-processing rates to multi-year lows and contributed to fuel shortages, raising gasoline prices and transportation costs across the economy. Inflation accelerated to an annualized 8% in Q3, from 4.8% in Q2, while core inflation rose to 6.2% from 4.5%. Annual inflation stood at an estimated 6.8% as of October 19 and is expected to reach 7.1–7.6% by year-end, above the central bank’s earlier forecast. The central bank said capacity constraints, strong wage growth and persistent inflation expectations pose significant risks, with proinflationary factors outweighing disinflationary ones. It expects inflation to return to its 4% target in 2027, projecting an average key rate of 12.5–13.5% that year.

【Research on Governance Framework for Intelligent Connected New Energy Vehicle Industry formally released】Oct 23 — At the closing of the 2026 World Intelligent Connected Vehicles Conference in Beijing, jointly hosted by the Ministry of Industry and Information Technology, the Ministry of Transport and the Beijing municipal government, the report Research on the Governance Framework for the Intelligent Connected New Energy Vehicle Industry under New Conditions was formally released. Led by the Equipment Industry Development Center and produced by a 13‑member team of OEMs, tech firms and academic institutions over one year, the report defines a seven‑element governance framework — objectives, principles, governed entities, stakeholders, tools, mechanisms and outcomes — and broadens governance from whole vehicles to algorithmic models, charging and battery‑swap facilities and software upgrade systems, while extending the governance chain across the full product lifecycle to better accommodate rapid technology iteration.

### 2026-10-23 18:28:27  #其他

【《新形勢下智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理體系框架研究》正式釋出】10月23日，由工業和資訊化部、交通運輸部、北京市人民政府聯合主辦的2026世界智慧網聯汽車大會閉幕式在北京召開。會上，《新形勢下智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理體系框架研究》（以下簡稱《報告》）正式釋出。《報告》由裝備工業發展中心牽頭，聯合整車企業、科技企業、高校機構等13家單位組成課題組，歷時一年完成。《報告》構建了涵蓋治理目標、原則、客體、主體、工具、機制和效果七個要素的智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理體系框架，將治理範圍從整車產品延伸至演算法模型、充換電設施、軟體升級系統等領域，將治理鏈條拓展到產品全生命週期，以更好適應技術快速迭代的特點。同時，《報告》從戰略引導與制度供給、跨部門跨央地協同、數字監管與風險防控、市場規範與社會共治、國際規則制定與協調五個方面提出能力提升路徑，推動制度在實踐中迭代完善，逐步形成創新協同、安全高效、持續演進、動態最佳化的行業現代化治理能力。下一步，裝備工業發展中心將持續深化智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理研究，與各方共同推動智慧網聯新能源汽車行業治理體系和治理能力現代化，在全球規則制定與協調中積極貢獻中國方案。

Bund Yields Near Two-Month Lows — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield eased to around 3.34%, touching its lowest level since August 31, as investors sought safe-haven assets amid mounting fiscal concerns and political uncertainty in France and other highly indebted eurozone economies. Meanwhile, fresh PMI data showed eurozone private-sector growth slowed to a four-month low in October, pressured by rising energy costs, higher interest rates and renewed contraction in France. Consumer confidence also deteriorated more sharply than expected. French bonds remained under particular pressure, with the spread between French OATs and German Bunds widening to around 170 basis points, its highest level since the eurozone sovereign debt crisis. Looking ahead, further demonstrations are expected across the region this weekend, while investors await next week’s inflation data and the ECB’s policy meeting, where officials are widely expected to leave interest rates unchanged.

UK Manufacturing Sector Grows at Faster Pace — The S&P Global UK Manufacturing PMI rose to 52.8 in October 2026, up from 51.9 in September and above market expectations of 51.7, according to the preliminary estimate. The reading signaled the strongest improvement in manufacturing business conditions since May, supported by the fastest growth in output in just over two years and a solid increase in new orders. However, employment declined slightly during the month. Meanwhile, supplier delivery times lengthened at the fastest pace since May, reflecting shipping delays and supply chain disruptions linked to the Middle East conflict, as well as global shortages of semiconductors and electronic components. Inventories continued to decline, with purchasing stocks falling for a fifth consecutive month. Input costs also rose sharply due to higher energy, raw material and transportation prices. Business confidence improved slightly, supported by hopes for AI investment and defense sector projects to boost future growth.

A number of firms cited a boost to sales from AI investment spending and rising demand for technology services. UK Business Activity Growth Picks Up to 8-Month High — The UK private sector continued to expand in October, with the flash Composite PMI Output Index rising to 53.1 from 52 in September, exceeding market expectations of 51.8 and reaching an eight-month high. Growth was supported by the sharpest increase in new business since September 2024. Both manufacturing and services recorded stronger output growth, while manufacturing activity posted its strongest increase in just over two years. Improving demand also helped employment return to growth for the first time since September 2024, ending a two-year period of job cuts, although the increase in staffing remained marginal. However, inflationary pressures intensified, with input costs rising sharply and the overall pace of cost inflation reaching its highest level since June. Business confidence regarding the year ahead edged higher in October, but optimism remained below the survey’s long-term average.

### 2026-10-23 16:33:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro area #Inflation Expectations #Importance 1

Euro Area Consumer Inflation Expectations at 4-Month High — Median expectations for price growth over the next 12 months in the Euro Area edged up to 3.1% in September 2026, the most in four months, compared to 3% in August. Consumers also expect the price of their home to increase by 3.5% over the next 12 months, higher than 3.4% in August. Expectations for mortgage interest rates in 12 months’ time remained unchanged at 4.9%. Meanwhile, median expectations for inflation three years ahead and five years ahead were unchanged at 2.9% and 2.5% respectively. Elsewhere, expectations for nominal income growth over the next 12 months increased to 1.1% from 1% and expectations for economic growth over the next 12 months decreased to -1.5% from -1.2% in August. Expectations for the unemployment rate 12 months ahead increased to 11.3% from 11%.

Taiwan Jobless Rate at 3.33% in September — Taiwan’s seasonally adjusted unemployment rate edged up by 0.01 percentage point from a month earlier to 3.33% in September 2026, as the number of unemployed people increased by 1 thousand to 401 thousand. Meanwhile, total employment declined by 4 thousand to 11.644 million. The labor force participation rate remained unchanged at 59.59%, with the total labor force falling by just 2 thousand to 12.046 million. Not seasonally adjusted, the unemployment rate ticked lower to 3.36% in September from 3.41% in August, with the number of unemployed people decreasing by 6 thousand to 405 thousand. Meanwhile, the youth unemployment rate among those aged 15-24 fell to 11.59% from 11.74% in the previous month.

### 2026-10-23 16:29:40  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Currency #Importance 1

Euro Falls on Weak PMI Data and Debt Concerns — The euro dropped to $1.12, remaining close to multi-month lows following the release of weaker-than-expected PMI data, while concerns over social unrest and rising public debt across Europe continued to weigh on the currency. Flash data showed eurozone private-sector growth slowed in October to a four-month low, pressured by rising energy costs, higher interest rates and renewed contraction in France amid political and fiscal uncertainty. Consumer confidence also deteriorated more sharply than expected. Meanwhile, investors remain concerned about rising borrowing costs across Europe, particularly in France, where 10-year government bond yields reached fresh 24-year highs and the risk premium climbed to levels last seen during the 2011 eurozone sovereign debt crisis. Looking ahead, further demonstrations are expected across the region this weekend, while investors await next week’s inflation data and the ECB’s policy meeting, where rates are widely expected to remain unchanged.

### 2026-10-23 16:14:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Composite PMI #Importance 2

Eurozone Business Activity Growth Slows — The S&P Global Eurozone Composite PMI fell to 51.9 in October 2026, down from September’s three-and-a-half-year high of 53.1 and below market expectations of 52.7, according to the preliminary estimate. Although business activity expanded for a fourth consecutive month, growth slowed to its weakest pace in the sequence, partly driven by a renewed contraction in French services activity. While strikes and unrest may have disrupted local economic activity in France, growth also eased across the rest of the eurozone, affecting both manufacturing and services. Meanwhile, new order growth weakened, bringing hiring close to stagnation, while rising energy prices drove input costs higher and prompted firms to increase selling prices. Higher interest rates also dampened demand, reinforcing concerns over slowing growth and persistent inflation that could fuel expectations of further ECB tightening. Finally, business confidence fell to a five-month low.

### 2026-10-23 16:12:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Euro Area Manufacturing Growth Slows to 3-Month Low — The S&P Global Eurozone Manufacturing PMI edged down to 52.4 in October 2026, its lowest level in three months, from 52.9 in September and below market expectations of 52.7. Manufacturing employment declined slightly following two consecutive months of growth. However, input purchasing continued to expand, with the pace accelerating to its joint-fastest in nearly four-and-a-half years, matching the rate recorded in April amid precautionary stockpiling linked to the ongoing conflict in the Middle East. As a result, manufacturers’ stocks of purchases increased for the first time in almost four years. Meanwhile, supplier lead times lengthened significantly, marking the sharpest deterioration in four months. On the price front, input costs rose sharply in October, driven by higher energy, fuel, and transportation costs. The increase was the steepest since May and exceeded the year-to-date average.

### 2026-10-23 16:02:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Nickel Retreats on Persistent Oversupply — Nickel traded around $15,900 per tonne, reversing recent gains as persistent global oversupply and subdued demand continued to constrain prices. Indonesia’s rapid expansion of nickel production has kept supply ample, while weaker stainless steel demand and the growing use of lithium iron phosphate batteries have limited consumption growth. The International Nickel Study Group expects the global surplus to widen in 2027, reinforcing the longer-term supply overhang. Meanwhile, reports of coordinated production cuts at Chinese-operated Indonesian smelters have raised hopes of tighter supply, with producers reportedly considering halving mixed hydroxide precipitate (MHP) output and cutting nickel pig iron production by 20%. Although the scale and timing of the measures remain uncertain, losses among Indonesian MHP producers strengthen the incentive to curb output, potentially tightening supply if the cuts are implemented.

### 2026-10-23 16:00:23  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

French Stocks Set for Weekly Decline — The CAC 40 fell 0.7% to 7,758 on Friday, extending losses from the previous session and heading for a weekly decline as investors weighed a batch of corporate earnings alongside PMI data. Luxury stocks were among the laggards, with Kering plunging 11.7% to over one-year low after Q3 2026 revenue came in flat year-on-year and down about 1% on a like-for-like basis. Hermès fell 1.1% to four-year low as its quarterly results disappointed investors amid challenging conditions in China. Safran also dropped 6.7% despite surpassing Q3 revenue growth forecasts. In contrast, L’Oréal climbed 3.5% to a more than two-year high after Q3 sales beat expectations. On the economic front, France’s flash composite PMI fell to 48.4 in October from 51.1 in September, well below forecasts of 50.5. Manufacturing PMI slipped to 49.3 from 50.6, returning to contraction and hitting an 11-month low. Services PMI also fell back into contraction, dropping to 48.4 from 51.2 and missing expectations of 50.7.

### 2026-10-23 15:59:10  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 1

DAX at 2-Week Low — Frankfurt's DAX 40 fell 0.6% to around 25,230 on Friday, hitting a two-week low and extending the previous session’s losses. While the earnings season continues to draw investors’ attention, sentiment remained cautious amid rising oil prices due to escalating tensions in the Middle East. Also, concerns over demonstrations scheduled for Saturday in France and Italy weighed. On the data front, flash PMI figures showed that Germany’s private sector continued to expand in October, though the pace of growth slowed more than expected. Most sectors traded lower, particularly telecom, software and autos, while tech outperformed. Among individual stocks, Deutsche Telekom led the losses, falling more than 3%, followed by MTU Aero Engines, Adidas, Continental and Airbus, which declined between 1.6% and 2.6%. On the upside, Rheinmetall (2.2%), Fresenius Medical Care (1.2%), Infineon Technologies (0.9%) and Vonovia (0.9%) posted the biggest gains. For the week, the index lost around 1.5%.

### 2026-10-23 15:53:09  #Trading Economics #Markets #Italy #Stock Market #Importance 0

FTSE MIB Extends Losses — Italy’s FTSE MIB extended its losses to around 48,300 on Friday, hovering near its lowest level since May, as losses in luxury stocks offset gains in energy shares. Eni climbed more than 3% after reporting third-quarter adjusted net profit of €2.8 billion, more than double the year-earlier figure, and announcing a €1.3 billion special dividend and an increase in its share buyback programme. Tenaris and Saipem also advanced 1.8% and 1.5%, respectively. On the downside, Moncler fell 2.1% and Brunello Cucinelli dropped 1.9%, as recent disappointing earnings from regional peers dampened hopes for a recovery in the sector. Automotive stock Stellantis also fell 2%. Investors also monitored developments in Italy’s banking sector ahead of an October 29 MPS shareholder meeting on its proposed merger with Mediobanca, amid reports of potential opposition from Banco BPM stakeholders. For the week, the benchmark index was on track to decline around 3%.

### 2026-10-23 15:51:41  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

UK Gas Prices Hit Fresh Multi-Year High Amid Tight Supply — UK natural gas prices climbed toward 230 pence per therm, reaching a fresh 2022 high amid tightening global supply. Ongoing disruptions to LNG supplies from the Middle East have driven British gas prices to three times their levels at the start of the year. Adding to concerns, gas output in the North Sea, which accounts for about a third of the nation’s winter demand, is projected to fall by about 5%, according to the UK’s gas-grid operator. To compensate for this, LNG imports are forecast to increase by 5%, while Norwegian pipeline deliveries are expected to rise by 1%. While the gas network operator indicated that the country would have adequate supply capacity even under worse-case scenarios, robust LNG demand could intensify competition for cargoes and push prices even higher. Meanwhile, additional production from the newly approved Jackdaw field may offer some relief, as it is expected to start supplying gas to the UK by the end of this month.

### 2026-10-23 15:50:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Poland #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Poland Jobless Rate Unchanged for 3rd Month — Poland’s registered unemployment rate stood at 5.8% in September 2026, in line with market expectations and unchanged for a third consecutive month. The latest result still marked the lowest level since December 2025, as the number of unemployed persons declined by 2.5 thousand from a month earlier to 907 thousand, with women representing 49.5% of the total. The majority of unemployed individuals were not entitled to unemployment benefits, accounting for 87.8% of the total. Meanwhile, 4.5% were out of work due to company-related terminations, while fresh graduates made up 3.4% of all unemployed persons. Additionally, 382.1 thousand had been out of work for more than a year. Compared with September 2025, the jobless rate was 0.2 percentage points higher, having stood at 5.6% a year earlier. Separately, reported job vacancies at the end of the month increased by 2.8 thousand to 47.2 thousand.

### 2026-10-23 15:44:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Services PMI #Importance 1

Germany Services Sector Growth Slows in October — Germany’s Services PMI fell to 51.7 in October 2026 from 52.9 in September, missing market expectations of 52.5, according to preliminary data. Although activity expanded for a second consecutive month, growth remained modest as demand stagnated amid delayed customer decisions and tighter client budgets. Services firms also reported a renewed decline in outstanding work. Despite easing capacity pressures at the start of the fourth quarter, companies continued to recruit, marking a third consecutive month of employment growth. However, job creation remained marginal and unchanged from September. On the price front, input cost inflation slowed, partly reflecting fuel discounts, while service providers moderated their own price increases amid competitive pressures. Looking ahead, business confidence in the services sector improved to its highest level since February, indicating stronger growth expectations despite subdued demand and limited momentum in current activity.

### 2026-10-23 15:43:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Composite PMI #Importance 1

German Private Sector Activity Growth Slows — The S&P Global Flash Germany Composite PMI fell to 52.8 in October 2026 from 53.8 in September, below forecasts of 53.3. The reading pointed to sustained, albeit slower, growth in private-sector business activity, amid a slowdown in both the services sector (51.7 vs. 52.9) and manufacturing output (55.1 vs. 56). New business continued to expand, but did so at the weakest rate for three months. Stronger growth expectations among services firms were offset by a notable reduction in confidence in the manufacturing sector, where input cost inflation surged to its highest since May. Amid reports of competitive pressures and discounted fuel prices, however, the overall rate of increase in prices charged for goods and services ticked down to a seven-month low. In addition, the pace of job creation at services firms was unchanged and only marginal. Workforce numbers in the manufacturing sector came close to stabilising, recording only a marginal fall that was the weakest for over three years.

### 2026-10-23 15:40:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Germany Manufacturing Sector Growth Beats Forecasts — Germany’s Manufacturing PMI rose to 54.2 in October 2026 from 53.9 in September, exceeding market expectations of 53.5, preliminary estimates showed. This marked a two-month high and signalled a continued improvement in manufacturing business conditions. However, manufacturing output growth eased, with the output index falling to 55.1 from 56.0, its lowest level in three months. Order books continued to expand, albeit at a slower pace, supported by sustained growth in export sales, while manufacturing backlogs recorded another robust rise. Meanwhile, employment edged closer to stabilisation, with job losses slowing to their weakest pace in over three years. On the price front, input-cost inflation accelerated for a second consecutive month to its highest level since May, driven by higher oil prices and rising raw material and component costs. Looking ahead, manufacturers’ business confidence weakened to its lowest level since July amid growing concerns over rising input prices.

FTSE 100 Set to End Week Lower — The FTSE 100 traded slightly lower on Friday, extending the previous session’s 0.3% decline and heading for a weekly loss of more than 1.5%. Rolls-Royce shares fell around 2.5% as the company reportedly neared a deal for up to £200 million in UK government subsidies to develop a new short-haul aircraft engine. AstraZeneca dropped over 1%, while GSK declined more than 0.5%.
